```
def corr_p_f(p, f):
    n = len(f)
    L = 1
    need = len(p)+n-1
    while L < need: L <<= 1
    R = np.zeros(L)
    R[:len(p)] = p[::-1]  # R[k]=p[len(p)-1-k]; if p length less than L, okay? For formula with R[k]=p_{L-1-k}, but using zero padding to left? 
```
Wait formula R[k]=p_{L-1-k} for k=0..L-1. If we set R[:len(p)] = p[::-1], that gives R[k]=p_{m-1-k} for k=0..m-1, and zeros after. But desired R has p at indices L-1-(0..m-1) = L-m .. L-1, not at start. Need either zero-pad at beginning:

```
For gradient, p weights sum 1, f sum1. Compute:
```
def smooth_weights(q, beta):
    q = np.asarray(q)
    qm = q.max()
    e = np.exp(beta*(q-qm))
    s = e.sum()
    if not finite or s==0: return np.ones(q.size)/q.size
    return e/s
```
For high beta, underflow; q-qm<=0 so okay. If all zeros of differences except active, weights uniform active.

`corr_p_f(p, f)`:
```
def corr_p_f(p, f):
    n=f.size
    m=p.size
    need=m+n-1
    L=1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    P=np.zeros(L); P[:m]=p[::-1]
    F=np.zeros(L); F[:n]=f
    C=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(P)*np.fft.rfft(F), L)[:need]
    # g_i = C[m-1 - i]
    g=C[m-1 : max(m-n, -1): -1]? simpler: g = C[n-1:m][:1?][::-1]
```
As above: `g = C[n-1:m][::-1]`? Need slice length: m=2n-1, stop m exclusive => indices 0..2n-2. Start n-1, stop 2n-1, length n. Good.
Return 2*g.
```
return 2.0 * C[n-1:m][::-1]
```

```
def convolve_fft_linear(x):
    n=x.size
    L=1; while L<2*n-1: L<<=1
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X,L)[:2*n-1]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0
    return c
```
Since x nonnegative, c nonnegative; numerical small negative okay. Use max.
```

```
`make_conv_template(n)`:
```
m=2*n-1
k = np.arange(m)
js = np.maximum(0, k - n + 1).astype(np.int32)
je = np.minimum(n-1, k).astype(np.int32)
counts = je - js + 1
total = counts.sum()
cum = np.cumsum(counts)
starts = np.repeat(cum-counts, counts)
local = np.arange(total)-starts
row = np.repeat(k, counts).astype(np.int32)
col_base = js[row.astype(np.int64)]
col = (col_base+local).astype(np.int32)
offsets = (row.astype(np.int64)-col.astype(np.int64)).astype(np.int32)
A_data = offsets.astype(np.float64)
A = csr_matrix((A_data, (row,col)), shape=(m,n))
# But duplicate entries? None.
edge_offsets = A.data.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
A.data.fill(0.0)
# After creating csr, data sorted maybe. Need ensure edge_offsets correspond to final data order. Use A.data after tocoo? We can get offsets from A.data if we construct with float offsets? Let's do:
coo = coo_matrix((offsets_float, (row,col)), shape=(m,n))
A = coo.tocsr()

```

LP `optimize_lp` new:
```
def optimize_lp(n, f_init, time_budget, rng, template=None):
   f=normalize; best_score=score_fft
   template prepared
   deadline...
   stall=0
   while time<deadline:
      b_f=convolve_fft(f); M=max(b_f)
      if M<=0 break
      res = solve_direction_lp(template, f, n, M, time_limit)
      if not success: if stall>... restart else continue
      x=res.x; clean; sum_s; if sum_s<=1+tol: restart/noise/stall
      g=x/sum_s
      # Also maybe improve by solving smaller sum? x is optimal max sum.
      # But if x has support causing b_g huge, line search maybe no improvement.
      c=conv_f_g; b_g=conv_g_g
      # safe t
      # line search, update if improves
      # if no improve, stochastic perturbation: f=mix_noisy(best_f, rng, 0.2)
   return best_f,best_score
```

Could improve Fourier sqrt by iterative target update:
```
def fourier_sqrt_poly(n, iterations=10, beta=0.7):
   b_target = uniform or current b flattened
   for it:
      f = sqrt_project(n,b_target)
      if None break
      b=convolve_fft(f)
      b_target = 0.5*b + 0.5*flatten(b_target?)
```
This alternating projection might converge to a decomposable low-peak sequence. We can implement a simple `_target_refine_candidate(n, initial_b, iterations, rng)`:
- b = target shape normalized (uniform or from current b flattened beta).
- for it in range(max_iters or time small):
   - f = fourier_sqrt_project_candidate(n,b)
   - if None break
   - score f; keep best
   - b_new = convolve_fft(f) maybe flatten and normalize: `b_new = b_new ** beta` (beta maybe 0.8)
   - b = (1-rho)*b + rho*b_new, normalize.
- Return best f.
This might be powerful. Each iteration O(L log L). For n=1024, L=4096, 100 iterations ~0.1s. Use as polynomial candidate generator.

Another generative method: "alternating projections" between f nonnegative length n and b low-peak decomposable (f*f). The square root step is a projection from b to f; the conv step from f to b. This is like fixed point. We'll include.
```

```
Let's implement details robustly.

normalize_sequence:
```
def normalize_sequence(arr):
    try: x = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    if x.size==0: return None
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    x /= s
    return x
```

next_pow2:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    p=1
    while p<x: p <<=1
    return p
```

convolve_fft:
```
def convolve_fft(x):
    n=x.size
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X,L)[:2*n-1]
    # clean tiny negative
    c = np.where(c > 1e-15, c, 0.0)
    return c
```
If x sum=1, sum c ~1. Good.
Could use `np.real`.

local_score:
```
def local_score(x):
    x=normalize_sequence(x)
    if x is None: return np.inf
    n=x.size
    b=convolve_fft(x)
    m=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m)
```
For speed in inner loops, use `score_from_b(b,n)` assuming b computed:
```
def score_from_b(b,n): m=np.max(b); return 2*n*m
```
Use local normalized.

softmax:
```
def softmax_from_log(logf):
    y=logf - np.max(logf)
    exp=np.exp(y)
    s=exp.sum()
    if not finite or s==0: return np.ones(len(logf))/len(logf)
    return exp/s
```

mix_noisy:
```
def mix_noisy(base,rng,eps):
    f=normalize_sequence(base)
    if f is None: return None
    n=f.size
    if eps<=1e-12: return f
    try: mix=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n))
    except: mix=np.ones(n)/n
    if eps>=1: f=mix
    else: f=(1-eps)*f + eps*mix
    return normalize_sequence(f)
```

interpolate_sequence:
```
def interpolate_sequence(x,n):
   x=normalize_sequence(x)
   if x is None or n<=0: return None
   m=x.size
   if m==n: return x
   if m==1: return np.ones(n)/n
   old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   out=np.interp(new, old, x)
   return normalize_sequence(out)
```

edge_sequence:
```
def edge_sequence(n, alpha=0.8, rng=None):
    i=np.arange(n)
    if n==1: return np.ones(1)
    # distance to edges: cos? high edges, low center: cos(pi*i/(n-1)) gives +1 edges, -1 center.
    base=1.0 + alpha*np.cos(np.pi*i/(n-1))
    if rng is not None:
       base = base + 0.05*rng.random(n)
    return normalize_sequence(base)
```
Maybe alpha up to 0.9 to keep positive.

dirichlet_sequence:
```
def dirichlet_sequence(n, rng, concentration=0.3):
    try: x=rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, concentration))
    except: x=np.ones(n)/n
    return normalize_sequence(x)

def append_unique(starts, f, tol=1e-5):
    f=normalize_sequence(f)
    if f is None or f.size==0: return
    for g in starts:
        if float(np.linalg.norm(f-g)) < tol: return
    starts.append(f)
```

target_flatten:
```
def target_flatten(b, beta=0.5):
    b=np.asarray(b,dtype=float)
    if b.size==0: return b
    b=np.where(np.isfinite(b),b,0.0); b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(b.size)/b.size
    b/=s
    if beta<=0: return b
    b=b**beta
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(b.size)/b.size
    return b/s
```
Could also uniform mix parameter.
```

def fourier_sqrt_project(n, b=None, L=None, top_windows=8):

       btarget=np.ones(m)/m

```
`fourier_alternating_candidate(n, rng, beta=0.7, max_iter=40, budget=?)`:
```
def fourier_alternating_candidate(n, rng, beta=0.7, max_iter=30, seed_b=None):
    m=2*n-1
    if seed_b is None or size mismatch: btarget=np.ones(m)/m
    else: btarget=target_flatten(seed_b, beta)
    best_f=None; best_s=inf
    deadline=time.monotonic()+5? Could pass budget_s
    for it in range(max_iter):
       f=fourier_sqrt_project(n, btarget, top_windows=4)
       if f is not None:
          s=local_score(f); if s<best_s: store
       b=convolve_fft(f) if f else uniform
       bflat=target_flatten(b, beta)
       btarget=0.5*btarget +0.5*bflat; normalize
    return best_f
```
Need budget maybe 5-10s. We'll call with time parameter.
This can be a start generator in make_starts, but calling multiple times may cost. We'll call once or twice.

`corr_p_f(p, f)` as derived with L next pow2 3n-2. But in gradient polish, p length 2n-1, f length n. Use:
```
def corr_p_f(p, f):
    n=f.size; m=p.size
    need=m+n-1
    L=next_pow2(need)
    P=np.zeros(L); P[:m]=p[::-1]
    F=np.zeros(L); F[:n]=f
    C=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(P)*np.fft.rfft(F), L)[:need]
    # g_i = C[m-1 - i]
    g = C[n-1:m][::-1]
    return 2.0*g
```
Check slice if m maybe not 2n-1? If p length different? We'll ensure p size 2n-1. If not, adjust: start = max(0,m-1-n+1)? But no.
For n=1, m=1, C[0:1][::-1], okay.

`smooth_weights(q,beta)`:
```
def smooth_weights(q,beta):
   q=np.asarray(q)
   qmax=float(np.max(q))
   if not finite: return ones
   if beta<=0: return ones/size
   e=np.exp(beta*(q-qmax))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return ones
   return e/s
```

Gradient polish implementation details:
Need avoid too many FFT if budget small. Use `deadline=start+budget`.
```
def gradient_polish(f, time_budget, rng, beta=500.0):
   f=normalize_sequence(f); if None return uniform
   n=f.size
   best_f=f.copy(); best_s=local_score(f)
   logf=np.log(f+1e-300); logf-=logf.mean()
   stall=0; beta_cur=beta
   deadline=...
   while time<deadline and stall<200:
       b=convolve_fft(f)
       bmax=float(np.max(b))
       if bmax<=0: break
       p=smooth_weights(b,beta_cur)
       gf=corr_p_f(p,f)
       # if gf norm tiny break
       # directions
       mean_wf=float(np.dot(f,gf))
       dy=f*(gf-mean_wf)
       dm=gf-mean_wf
       # Also maybe raw gf - np.mean(gf)
       best_cand=None; cand_score=best_s
       # scale factors for dy and dm
       norm_dy=float(np.linalg.norm(dy))
       norm_dm=float(np.linalg.norm(dm))
       base_dy = 1.0/(norm_dy+1e-12)
       base_dm = 1.0/(norm_dm+1e-12)
       # But for dy, entries ~1/n^2, base~n^{3/2}; may be good. For n=1024 base~33k? Wait norm_dy maybe 1/n^{3/2}=0.03, base 33. Not 33k. OK.
       mults=[1e-3,1e-2,5e-2,0.1,0.5,1,5,20,100]
       # To limit, if base huge, multipliers up to 100 -> large logs; softmax safe.
       for dir, base in ((dy, base_dy), (dm, base_dm)):
          if not finite base or base<=0: continue
          for mu in mults:
             lr=base*mu
             if lr<=0 or not finite: continue
             new_log=logf - lr*dir
             new_f=softmax_from_log(new_log)
             sc=local_score(new_f) # This does FFT; could use b? no
             if sc<cand_score - 1e-12:
                cand_score=sc; best_cand=new_f; best_log=new_log.copy()
       if best_cand is not None:
          f=best_cand; logf=best_log; best_f=f.copy(); best_s=cand_score; stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall % 10 ==0:
             # change beta: alternate lower/higher
             if beta_cur < 1e6: beta_cur *=2.0
             else: beta_cur /=4.0
          if stall % 50 ==0:
             f=mix_noisy(best_f, rng, 0.1+0.1*(stall//50)%3)
             if f is None: f=best_f.copy()
             logf=np.log(f+1e-300)-np.log(f+1e-300).mean(); best_s? keep best_s but f score maybe worse
             if stall>200: break
   return best_f, best_s
```

`make_conv_template`:
```
def make_conv_template(n):
    m=2*n-1
    k=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    js=np.maximum(0, k - n + 1).astype(np.int64)
    je=np.minimum(n - 1, k).astype(np.int64)
    counts=je - js + 1
    total=int(counts.sum())
    cum=counts.cumsum()
    indptr=np.zeros(m+1, dtype=np.int32)
    indptr[1:]=cum.astype(np.int32)
    starts=np.repeat(cum - counts, counts)
    local=np.arange(total, dtype=np.int64) - starts
    row=np.repeat(k, counts).astype(np.int64)
    col=(js[row] + local).astype(np.int32)
    offsets=(row - col).astype(np.float64)
    A=csr_matrix((offsets, col, indptr), shape=(m,n))
    edge_offsets=A.data.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    A.data.fill(0.0)
    obj=-np.ones(n)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
    return A, edge_offsets, obj, bounds, m
```
Memory: `row`, `starts`, etc int64 8M*8=64MB each, multiple arrays maybe high. For n=2048, total 8.3M, arrays row int64 66MB, col int32 33, offsets float 66, cum small. Total perhaps 200MB, okay. We can reduce by using int32 where possible and avoid row int64? Need row for offset = row-col can be int32 (<=n). Use int32 arrays. `np.repeat(k, counts)` with k int32? `k` up to4095 int32. `counts` int32? total 8M fits. Implement with int32 to save memory.
```
k=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int32)
js=np.maximum(0, k.astype(np.int64?) - n +1) but n up to 2048, int32 safe.
```
Use int32 for all except cum indptr maybe int32. For row - col within [-2048,4096] int32. Good. `np.arange(total)` int64 needed? total 8M fits int32. Use int32. But `np.repeat(cum-counts, counts)` with cum int32 okay. Let's write simple with int64 and delete; memory likely okay but to be safe use int32.
```
k = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int32)
js = np.maximum(0, k.astype(np.int64) - n + 1).astype(np.int32) # temporary int64 length 4095 small
je = np.minimum(n - 1, k.astype(np.int64)).astype(np.int32)
counts = (je.astype(np.int64) - js.astype(np.int64) + 1).astype(np.int64) # small length
total = int(counts.sum())
cum64 = np.cumsum(counts)
indptr = np.zeros(m+1, dtype=np.int32); indptr[1:] = cum64.astype(np.int32)
starts = np.repeat(cum64 - counts, counts).astype(np.int32)
local = (np.arange(total, dtype=np.int32) - starts)
row = np.repeat(k, counts).astype(np.int32)
col = (js[row] + local).astype(np.int32)
data = (row - col).astype(np.float64)
A = csr_matrix((data, col, indptr), shape=(m,n))
edge_offsets = A.data.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
A.data.fill(0.0)
```
`np.repeat` with counts int64 returns int32 if input int32? okay.

`solve_direction_lp`:
```
def solve_direction_lp(template, f, n, M, time_limit):
   A, edge_offsets, obj, bounds, m = template
   scale=float(n)
   A.data[:] = f[edge_offsets]
   A.data *= scale
   b = np.full(m, M*scale*scale, dtype=np.float64)
   options={'presolve': True, 'time_limit': float(max(1.0, time_limit))}
   try:
      res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=options)
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None: return None
   if res.status not in (0,1,2): return None
   x=res.x
   # We should verify primal feasibility? If status 1 but x feasible maybe okay. Check max(A@x - b) small.
   try:
       # A is sparse, use reshape? A.dot(x)
       av=A.dot(x)
       if np.max(av - b) > 1e-4 * max(1.0, float(np.max(b))):
           # maybe still use if violation small? return None
           if np.max(av - b) > 1e-2 * max(1.0, float(np.max(b))): return None
   except Exception:
       pass
   x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x,0.0); x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
   return x

Need `make_safe_ts`:
```
def make_safe_ts(b_f,c,b_g,M):
    alpha=2.0*(c-M)
    beta=b_g+b_f-2.0*c
    # Due to numerics, alpha may >0 small; set root=0 if alpha>=-tol.
    tol=1e-10*max(1.0,M)
    roots=np.full(c.size, 1.0)
    pos=beta>1e-14
    # for pos, if alpha < -tol: root=-alpha/beta else 0
    r=np.zeros(c.size)
    r[pos] = np.where(alpha[pos] < -tol, -alpha[pos]/beta[pos], 0.0)
    roots[pos]=np.minimum(roots[pos], r[pos])
    roots=np.maximum(roots,0.0)
    t_safe=float(np.min(roots))
    if not finite or t_safe <=1e-8: return []
    t_safe=min(1.0, t_safe*0.999999)
    # make grid
    lo=max(1e-8, t_safe*1e-6)
    if t_safe < 1e-8: return []
    ts=[]
    # log grid
    try: ts.append(np.logspace(math.log10(lo), math.log10(t_safe), 12))
    except: ts=[]
    # linear grid
    ts.append(np.linspace(lo, t_safe, 12))
    # add t_safe*0.5, 0.05 etc
    ts_list = np.unique(np.concatenate(ts))
    ts_list=ts_list[(ts_list>0)&(ts_list<=t_safe)]
    return ts_list.tolist()
```
No lambda. Use loops? `np.unique` ok.

```
def fourier_alternating_candidate(n, rng, beta=0.7, max_iter=40, seed_b=None, budget_s=8.0):
    m=2*n-1
    if seed_b is None:
       btarget=np.ones(m)/m
    else:
       btarget=np.asarray(seed_b,dtype=float).ravel()
       if btarget.size != m: # resize
           old=np.linspace(0,1,btarget.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,m); btarget=np.interp(new,old,btarget)
       btarget=target_flatten(btarget, beta)
    best_f=None; best_s=np.inf
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,budget_s)
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2: break
        f=fourier_sqrt_project(n, btarget, L=None, top_windows=4)
        if f is not None:
            s=local_score(f)
            if s<best_s:
                best_s=s; best_f=f.copy()
        if f is None: break
        b=convolve_fft(f)
        bflat=target_flatten(b, beta)
        btarget=0.5*btarget + 0.5*bflat
        ssum=btarget.sum()
        if ssum<=0: btarget=np.ones(m)/m
        else: btarget/=ssum
    if best_f is None:
        best_f=fourier_sqrt_project(n, btarget, top_windows=4)
    return best_f
```

```
def search_length(n, budget, deadline, rng, best_f, best_score, given_f, lp_frac=0.5):
   t0=time.monotonic()
   local_deadline=min(float(deadline), t0+float(budget))
   starts=make_starts(n,rng,best_f,given_f)
   if not starts: starts=[np.ones(n)/float(n)]
   lp_budget=budget*lp_frac
   if lp_budget>20 and n<=MAX_LP_N:
      lp_deadline=min(local_deadline, t0+lp_budget)
      template=None
      try: template=make_conv_template(n)
      except Exception as e: print...
      if template is not None:
         num=min(3,len(starts))
         if num==1: weights=[1.0]
         elif num==2: weights=[0.6,0.4]
         else: weights=[0.5,0.3,0.2]
         for idx in range(num):
             now=time.monotonic()
             if now >= lp_deadline-2: break
             tb=lp_budget*weights[idx]
             tb=min(tb, lp_deadline-now-1.0)
             if tb<3.0: continue
             init=starts[idx]
             # if best improved, maybe replace init by upsample best? optional
             f_res,s_res=optimize_lp(n, init, tb, rng, template)
             if np.isfinite(s_res) and s_res<best_score:
                 best_score=s_res; best_f=np.asarray(f_res, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                 print...
         try: del template
   # gradient phase
   grad_deadline=local_deadline
   now=time.monotonic()
   if now < grad_deadline-10:
       init=interpolate_sequence(best_f,n)
       if init is None: init=starts[0]
       tb=min(300.0, grad_deadline-now-5.0)
       if tb>5.0:
           f_res,s_res=gradient_polish(init,tb,rng)
           update
   now=time.monotonic()
   if now < grad_deadline-20 and len(starts)>1:
       for idx in range(1,min(3,len(starts))):
          now=...
          if now >= grad_deadline-10: break
          init=starts[idx]
          # maybe if too similar to best skip
          tb=min(120.0, grad_deadline-now-5.0)
          f_res,s_res=gradient_polish(init,tb,rng)
          update
   return best_f,best_score
```

Let's draft code:

```
def normalize_sequence(arr):
    try:
        x = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if x.size == 0:
        return None
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    x = x / s
    return x
```

`next_pow2` as above.

`convolve_fft`:
```
def convolve_fft(x):
    n = x.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, L)[:2*n - 1]
    c = c.real
    c[c < 1e-15] = 0.0
    return c
```
For very small negative large? Clip all negative to 0: `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`. But if numerical negative significant? no. Use `np.maximum(c,0)`.

`cross_convolve_fft`:
```
def cross_convolve_fft(a,b):
   n=a.size; m=b.size
   L=next_pow2(n+m-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)[:n+m-1]
   return np.maximum(c.real,0.0)
```

Score:
```
def score_normalized(x):
   n=x.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   s=float(np.sum(x))
   if s<=0: return np.inf
   b=convolve_fft(x)
   m=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
   if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))

def local_score(x):
   x=normalize_sequence(x)
   if x is None: return np.inf
   return score_normalized(x)
```

Softmax:
```
def softmax_from_log(logf):
   y=logf - float(np.max(logf))
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.ones(logf.size, dtype=np.float64)/float(logf.size)
   return e/s
```

Mix, interpolate, edge, dirichlet as above.

`append_unique`:
```
def append_unique(starts, f, tol=1e-5):
    f=normalize_sequence(f)
    if f is None or f.size==0: return
    for g in starts:
        if float(np.linalg.norm(f-g)) < tol: return
    starts.append(f)
```
If n large (8192), norm O8192 fine.

`target_flatten` as above.

`fourier_sqrt_project` careful with `local_score` uses FFT. In start selection, evaluating both pos and abs windows maybe many. Use `top_windows` param.
```
def fourier_sqrt_project(n, b=None, top_windows=4):
    m=2*n-1
    if b is None:
        btarget=np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)/float(m)
    else:
        bt=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if bt.size == 0: bt=np.ones(m)/m
        elif bt.size != m:
            old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,bt.size); new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); bt=np.interp(new,old,bt)
        bt=np.where(np.isfinite(bt),bt,0.0); bt=np.maximum(bt,0.0)
        s=bt.sum()
        if s<=0: bt=np.ones(m)/m
        else: bt=s? Actually bt/=s
        btarget=bt
    L=next_pow2(max(4*n, 2*m+16))
    bL=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64); bL[:m]=btarget
    B=np.fft.fft(bL)
    F=np.empty(L, dtype=np.complex128)
    F[0]=np.sqrt(max(float(np.real(B[0])),0.0))
    half=L//2
    if L %2 ==0:
       vals=np.sqrt(B[1:half])
       F[1:half]=vals
       F[half+1:L]=np.conj(vals[::-1])
       Bh=float(np.real(B[half]))
       F[half]=np.sqrt(Bh) if Bh>=0 else 0.0
    else:
       vals=np.sqrt(B[1:(L+1)//2])
       F[1:(L+1)//2]=vals
       rev=vals[::-1]
       F[(L+1)//2:L]=np.conj(rev[::-1])? Need for odd easier but L power 2 even. So ignore.
    f_full=np.fft.ifft(F).real
    # if all zero fallback?
    pos=np.maximum(f_full,0.0)
    psum=float(np.sum(pos))
    if psum <= 1e-300:
        # try absolute full clipped
        seg=np.abs(f_full)
        return normalize_sequence(seg[:n])
    full_tiled=np.tile(f_full, 2)
    pe=np.tile(pos, 2)
    cum=np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), np.cumsum(pe)))
    sums=cum[n:L+n] - cum[:L]
    idx_list=[0, int(np.argmax(sums))]
    top_idx=np.argsort(sums)[-max(1,top_windows):]
    for idx in top_idx: idx_list.append(int(idx))
    seen=[]
    for idx in idx_list:
       idx=int(idx) % L
       if idx not in seen: seen.append(idx)
    best_f=None; best_s=np.inf
    for idx in seen:
       seg=full_tiled[idx:idx+n].real
       cand=normalize_sequence(np.maximum(seg,0.0))
       if cand is not None:
          sc=local_score(cand)
          if sc < best_s:
             best_s=sc; best_f=cand.copy()
       cand2=normalize_sequence(np.abs(seg))
       if cand2 is not None:
          sc2=local_score(cand2)
          if sc2 < best_s:
             best_s=sc2; best_f=cand2.copy()
    if best_f is None:
       best_f=normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f_full[:n],0.0))
    return best_f
```
For L even always; power of 2 even unless L=1 but n large. Good.
`np.tile(f_full,2)` memory 2L ok (n=8192 L=32768 -> 65536 complex? f_full real 8 bytes 512KB; okay). `np.tile(pos,2)` same.
Potential bug: `F[half+1:L] = np.conj(vals[::-1])` length: vals length half-1. Slice L-half to L length half. Wait for L=8, half=4, slice half+1=5 to L=8 length3, vals[::-1] length3, ok indices5,6,7. Good. For L=4, half=2, vals length1 (index1), slice3:4 length1 (index3), good; Nyquist index2 assigned. For L=2, half=1, B[1:1] empty, slice2:2 empty, Nyquist index1 assigned. Fine.
What about B values with branch cut: If B[k] is near negative real, sqrt imaginary. Fine.

```
def fourier_alternating_candidate(n, rng, beta=0.6, max_iter=20, budget_s=4.0, seed_b=None):
   m=2*n-1
   if seed_b is None: btarget=np.ones(m)/m
   else: ... as in project
   best_f=None; best_s=np.inf

   if max_iter <=0: max_iter=1
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2: break
      f=fourier_sqrt_project(n, btarget, top_windows=3)
      if f is None: break
      sc=local_score(f)
      if sc < best_s: ...
      b=convolve_fft(f)
      bflat=target_flatten(b,beta)
      btarget=0.5*btarget+0.5*bflat
      s=bt sum; if s<=0 uniform else /s
   if best_f is None: best_f=fourier_sqrt_project(n,btarget,top_windows=3)
   return best_f
```

```
def make_starts(n, rng, best_f, given_f):
   starts=[]
   f=interpolate_sequence(best_f,n)
   if f is not None:
      f=mix_noisy(f,rng,0.03)
      append_unique(starts,f)
   if given_f is not None:
      f=interpolate_sequence(given_f,n)
      if f is not None:
         f=mix_noisy(f,rng,0.05)
         append_unique(starts,f)
   f=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
   f=mix_noisy(f,rng,0.8)
   append_unique(starts,f)
   f=edge_sequence(n,0.7,rng)
   append_unique(starts,f)
   f=dirichlet_sequence(n,rng,0.3)
   append_unique(starts,f)
   f=fourier_sqrt_project(n,None,top_windows=3)
   if f is not None:
      f=mix_noisy(f,rng,0.02)
      append_unique(starts,f)
   if best_f is not None:
      fb=interpolate_sequence(best_f,n)
      if fb is not None:
         b=convolve_fft(fb)
         max_iter=max(4, min(25, 2500//max(1,n)))
         f=fourier_alternating_candidate(n,rng,beta=0.6,max_iter=max_iter,budget_s=3.0,seed_b=b)
         if f is not None:
            f=mix_noisy(f,rng,0.02)
            append_unique(starts,f)
   scored=[]
   for f in starts:
      sc=local_score(f)
      if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,f))
   if not scored:
      return [np.ones(n)/float(n)]
   scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
   out=[]
   for sc,f in scored[:6]: out.append(f)
   return out
```

`optimize_lp` detailed:
```
def optimize_lp(n, f_init, time_budget, rng, template):
   f=normalize_sequence(f_init)
   if f is None or f.size != n:
      f=interpolate_sequence(f_init,n)
   if f is None: f=np.ones(n)/n
   if template is None:
      try: template=make_conv_template(n)
      except: return f, score_normalized(f)
   best_f=f.copy(); best_s=score_normalized(f)
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0.0,float(time_budget))
   stall=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      it+=1
      rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if rem < 4.0: break
      b_f=convolve_fft(f)
      M=float(np.max(b_f))
      if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0: break
      x=solve_direction_lp(template,f,n,M,min(90.0, rem-2.0))
      if x is None:
         stall+=1
         if stall > 4: break
         f=mix_noisy(best_f, rng, 0.3)
         if f is None: f=best_f.copy()
         continue
      s_star=float(np.sum(x))
      if s_star <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
         stall+=1
         if stall > 10: break
         eps=min(0.7,0.2+0.1*stall)
         f=mix_noisy(best_f, rng, eps)
         if f is None: f=best_f.copy()
         continue
      g=x/s_star
      g=normalize_sequence(g)
      if g is None: ...
      c=cross_convolve_fft(f,g)
      b_g=convolve_fft(g)
      ts=make_safe_ts(b_f,c,b_g,M)
      best_cand=None; best_cand_s=best_s; best_log? no
      # line search
      if ts:
         Ts=np.asarray(ts, dtype=np.float64)
         # ensure finite
         vals = ((1.0-Ts)[:,None]**2) * b_f[None,:] + (2.0*Ts*(1.0-Ts))[:,None] * c[None,:] + (Ts[:,None]**2) * b_g[None,:]
         maxvals=np.max(vals, axis=1)
         scores=2.0*float(n)*maxvals
         idx=int(np.argmin(scores))
         cand_score=float(scores[idx]); t=float(Ts[idx])
         f_new=(1.0-t)*f + t*g
         f_new=normalize_sequence(f_new)
         if f_new is not None:
             sc=score_normalized(f_new)
             if sc < best_cand_s:
                 best_cand_s=sc; best_cand=f_new
      # also consider g and half
      sc_g=score_normalized(g)
      if sc_g < best_cand_s:
          best_cand_s=sc_g; best_cand=g.copy()
      half=0.5*f+0.5*g; half=normalize_sequence(half)
      if half is not None:
          sc_half=score_normalized(half)
          if sc_half < best_cand_s:
              best_cand_s=sc_half; best_cand=half
      if best_cand is not None and best_cand_s < best_s - 1e-12:
          f=best_cand.copy()
          best_f=f.copy(); best_s=best_cand_s; stall=0
          if it <=5 or it %20 ==0:
              print(f"     n={n} LP iter={it} score={best_s:.6f} s_star={s_star:.4f}", flush=True)
      else:
          stall+=1
          # move toward g a little even if no? Maybe not
          f=mix_noisy(best_f, rng, min(0.5,0.15+0.05*stall))
          if f is None: f=best_f.copy()
   return best_f, float(best_s)
```

`make_safe_ts` implementation with `math.log10` if lo positive. Use `math` imported.
```
def make_safe_ts(b_f,c,b_g,M):
   m=c.size
   tol=1e-10*max(1.0, float(M))
   alpha=2.0*(c-M)
   beta=b_g+b_f-2.0*c
   roots=np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
   pos=beta > 1e-14
   if np.any(pos):
       ap=alpha[pos]; bp=beta[pos]
       r=np.where(ap < -tol, -ap/bp, 0.0)
       roots[pos]=np.minimum(roots[pos], r)
   roots=np.maximum(roots,0.0)
   t_safe=float(np.min(roots))
   if not np.isfinite(t_safe) or t_safe <= 1e-8:
       return []
   if t_safe > 1.0: t_safe=1.0
   t_safe = t_safe * 0.999999
   lo=max(1e-8, t_safe*1e-6)
   if lo >= t_safe: lo=t_safe*0.5
   ts_parts=[]
   ts_parts.append(np.logspace(math.log10(lo), math.log10(t_safe), 12))
   ts_parts.append(np.linspace(lo, t_safe, 12))
   ts_arr=np.unique(np.concatenate(ts_parts))
   ts_arr=ts_arr[(ts_arr>0.0) & (ts_arr<=t_safe)]
   return [float(v) for v in ts_arr]
```
No lambda. List comprehension uses variable v, okay. If `math.log10(t_safe)` with t_safe 1e-9 okay. `np.logspace` requires start<stop; if lo close to t_safe, we adjust. Use try/except.

```
def gradient_polish(f_init, time_budget, rng, beta_hint=0.0):

   if f is None: f=np.ones(max(2,int(f_init.size?)))? If f_init None? handle.

   mults=np.array([1e-3,1e-2,3e-2,1e-1,3e-1,1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0], dtype=np.float64)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:

       if current_beta > 0 and beta_cur < current_beta? maybe use max? We can set beta_cur=current_beta? Let's combine: beta_cur=max(beta_cur, current_beta*0.5). Simpler dynamic `beta = max(1000, 20/bmax, current_beta)`, and adapt current_beta.
       p=smooth_weights(b, beta)
       gf=corr_p_f(p,f)
       if not finite norm: break

       s1=float(np.dot(f,d1))

       d2 = d1  # multiplicative
       best_cand=None; best_cand_s=best_s

          dir=dir - float(np.dot(f,dir))
          norm_dir=float(np.linalg.norm(dir))
          if not finite or norm_dir <= 1e-300: continue
          base=1.0/norm_dir
          # also scale with 1/max abs? base maybe too small if norm dominated few. Add base2=1/max(abs)
          max_abs=float(np.max(np.abs(dir)))

             if lr<=0 or not finite: continue
             new_log=logf - lr*dir
             new_log -= float(np.mean(new_log))
             new_f=softmax_from_log(new_log)

             if sc < best_cand_s - 1e-12:

       if best_cand is not None:

          if stall? print every 50
       else:

   return best_f, float(best_s)
```

`smooth_weights`:
```
def smooth_weights(q,beta):
   q=np.asarray(q,dtype=float)
   if q.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   qmax=float(np.max(q))
   if not np.isfinite(qmax): return np.ones(q.size)/q.size
   if beta<=0: return np.ones(q.size)/q.size
   e=np.exp(np.clip(beta*(q-qmax), -700, 0.0))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(q.size)/q.size
   return e/s
```
Clip to avoid underflow; q-qmax ≤0, but floating maybe >0 small, clip at 0. Use `np.minimum(0, beta*(q-qmax))`.

`corr_p_f` as above.

```
def prepare_sequence(f):
   arr=np.asarray(f,float)
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<=0 or arr.size==0: return [1.0/64.0]*64
   arr=arr/s
   out=[]
   for v in arr:
      if v<0: v=0.0
      if v>1000.0: v=1000.0
      out.append(float(v))
   return out
```

```
   i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   center=n-1
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(f,f)) / sum(f)^2.

The program combines:
  - LP best-response local search (maximize sum(g) subject to f*g <= M),
  - FFT-based smooth/pseudo-gradient polishing on the simplex,
  - Fourier square-root / flattened-transformative candidates to jump basins,
  - multiple sequence lengths and random starts.

The global variable height_sequence_1 is used as a parent construction when
available.  The function propose_candidate() is the required entry point.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np

from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from operator import itemgetter


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_sequence(arr):
    """Return a non-negative float array normalized to sum 1, or None."""
    try:
        x = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if x.size == 0:
        return None
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return x / s


def next_pow2(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x, as int."""
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def convolve_fft(x):
    """Linear self-convolution of x via FFT. x must be 1D."""
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X * X, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = c.real
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def cross_convolve_fft(a, b):
    """Linear convolution of two 1D arrays via FFT."""
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_pow2(n + m - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, L)[:n + m - 1]
    c = c.real
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_normalized(x):
    """Score for a non-negative array, accounting for its sum."""
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    b = convolve_fft(x)
    if b.size == 0:
        m = 0.0
    else:
        m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def local_score(x):
    """Score after normalizing to sum 1 and non-negativity."""
    x = normalize_sequence(x)
    if x is None:
        return float("inf")
    return score_normalized(x)


def softmax_from_log(logf):
    """Stable softmax."""
    y = np.asarray(logf, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(y.size)
    return e / s


def mix_noisy(base, rng, eps):
    """Mix base with a Dirichlet-noise vector."""
    f = normalize_sequence(base)
    if f is None:
        return None
    n = f.size
    if eps <= 1e-12:
        return f
    try:
        mix = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        mix = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if eps >= 1.0:
        f = mix
    else:
        f = (1.0 - eps) * f + eps * mix
    return normalize_sequence(f)


def interpolate_sequence(x, n):
    """Resample a non-negative sequence to length n and normalize."""
    x = normalize_sequence(x)
    if x is None or n <= 0:
        return None
    m = x.size
    if m == int(n):
        return x
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(new, old, x)
    return normalize_sequence(y)


def append_unique(starts, f, tol=1e-5):
    """Append f to starts if not already too close to an existing element."""
    f = normalize_sequence(f)
    if f is None or f.size == 0:
        return
    for g in starts:
        if float(np.linalg.norm(f - g)) < tol:
            return
    starts.append(f)


def binomial_sqrt_sequence(n):
    """Positive coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2): binom(2k,k)/4^k, length n."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        x[i] = x[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return normalize_sequence(x)


def edge_sequence(n, alpha=0.7, rng=None):
    """Sequence high near both ends and low near the middle."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = 1.0 + float(alpha) * np.cos(np.pi * i / float(n - 1))
    if rng is not None:
        base = base + 0.05 * rng.random(n)
    return normalize_sequence(base)


def two_bump_sequence(n, sigma_frac=0.12, rng=None):
    """Two Gaussian bumps at the two edges plus a small floor."""
    n = int(max(2, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    center = float(n - 1)
    d = np.minimum(i, center - i)
    s = max(1.0, float(sigma_frac) * center)
    x = np.exp(-(d / s) ** 2) + 1e-6
    if rng is not None:
        x = x + 0.05 * rng.random(n)
    return normalize_sequence(x)


def dirichlet_sequence(n, rng, concentration=0.3):
    """Random Dirichlet sequence."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    try:
        x = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, concentration, dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        x = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return normalize_sequence(x)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fourier square-root based candidates
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def target_flatten(b, beta=0.5):
    """Flatten a non-negative coefficient vector and normalize it."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b.size == 0:
        return b
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(b.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(b.size)
    b = b / s
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return b
    b = np.power(b, beta)
    s2 = float(b.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(b.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(b.size)
    return b / s2


def fourier_sqrt_project(n, b=None, top_windows=4):
    """
    Project a target convolution b to a non-negative length-n sequence by
    taking a conjugate-symmetric Fourier square root, then selecting a
    promising length-n window and clipping to nonnegative.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1

    if b is None:
        btarget = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        bt = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if bt.size == 0:
            bt = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        elif bt.size != m:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, bt.size, dtype=np.float64)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
            bt = np.interp(new, old, bt)
        bt = np.where(np.isfinite(bt), bt, 0.0)
        bt = np.maximum(bt, 0.0)
        s = float(bt.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            bt = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        else:
            bt = bt / s
        btarget = bt

    L = next_pow2(max(4 * n, 2 * m + 16))
    bL = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    bL[:m] = btarget

    B = np.fft.fft(bL)
    F = np.empty(L, dtype=np.complex128)

    # Enforce conjugate symmetry, choosing a consistent square-root branch.
    F[0] = np.sqrt(max(float(np.real(B[0])), 0.0))
    half = L // 2
    if L % 2 == 0:
        vals = np.sqrt(B[1:half])
        F[1:half] = vals
        F[half + 1:L] = np.conj(vals[::-1])
        Bh = float(np.real(B[half]))
        F[half] = np.sqrt(Bh) if Bh >= 0.0 else 0.0
    else:
        # L is always a power of two here, but keep a fallback.
        mid = (L + 1) // 2
        vals = np.sqrt(B[1:mid])
        F[1:mid] = vals
        F[mid:L] = np.conj(vals[::-1])

    f_full = np.fft.ifft(F).real
    pos = np.maximum(f_full, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(pos)) <= 1e-300:
        return normalize_sequence(np.abs(f_full[:n]))

    full_tiled = np.concatenate((f_full, f_full))
    pos_tiled = np.concatenate((pos, pos))
    cum = np.empty(L * 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cum[0] = 0.0
    cum[1:] = np.cumsum(pos_tiled)
    sums = cum[n:L + n] - cum[:L]

    idx_list = [0, int(np.argmax(sums))]
    top_idx = np.argsort(sums)[-max(1, int(top_windows)):]
    for idx in top_idx:
        idx_list.append(int(idx))

    seen = []
    for idx in idx_list:
        idx = int(idx) % L
        if idx not in seen:
            seen.append(idx)

    best_f = None
    best_s = float("inf")
    for idx in seen:
        seg = full_tiled[idx:idx + n].real

        cand = normalize_sequence(np.maximum(seg, 0.0))
        if cand is not None:
            sc = local_score(cand)
            if sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_f = cand.copy()

        cand2 = normalize_sequence(np.abs(seg))
        if cand2 is not None:
            sc2 = local_score(cand2)
            if sc2 < best_s:
                best_s = sc2
                best_f = cand2.copy()

    if best_f is None:
        best_f = normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f_full[:n], 0.0))
    return best_f


def fourier_alternating_candidate(n, rng, beta=0.6, max_iter=20,
                                  budget_s=4.0, seed_b=None):
    """
    Alternating projections:
        target b -> Fourier sqrt projection to f -> b = f*f (flattened).
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1

    if seed_b is None:
        btarget = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        bt = np.asarray(seed_b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if bt.size == 0:
            bt = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        elif bt.size != m:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, bt.size, dtype=np.float64)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
            bt = np.interp(new, old, bt)
        bt = np.where(np.isfinite(bt), bt, 0.0)
        bt = np.maximum(bt, 0.0)
        s = float(bt.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            bt = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        else:
            bt = bt / s
        btarget = target_flatten(bt, beta)

    best_f = None
    best_s = float("inf")
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    for _ in range(max(1, int(max_iter))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.2:
            break

        f = fourier_sqrt_project(n, btarget, top_windows=3)
        if f is None:
            break

        sc = local_score(f)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
            best_s = sc
            best_f = f.copy()

        b = convolve_fft(f)
        bflat = target_flatten(b, beta)
        btarget = 0.5 * btarget + 0.5 * bflat
        s = float(btarget.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            btarget = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        else:
            btarget = btarget / s

    if best_f is None:
        best_f = fourier_sqrt_project(n, btarget, top_windows=3)
    return best_f


def make_starts(n, rng, best_f, given_f):
    """Build a small diverse pool of initial sequences of length n."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    starts = []

    if best_f is not None:
        f = interpolate_sequence(best_f, n)
        if f is not None:
            f = mix_noisy(f, rng, 0.03)
            append_unique(starts, f)

    if given_f is not None:
        f = interpolate_sequence(given_f, n)
        if f is not None:
            f = mix_noisy(f, rng, 0.05)
            append_unique(starts, f)

    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    f = mix_noisy(f, rng, 0.8)
    append_unique(starts, f)

    f = edge_sequence(n, alpha=0.7, rng=rng)
    append_unique(starts, f)

    f = two_bump_sequence(n, sigma_frac=0.12, rng=rng)
    append_unique(starts, f)

    f = dirichlet_sequence(n, rng, concentration=0.3)
    append_unique(starts, f)

    f = binomial_sqrt_sequence(n)
    append_unique(starts, f)

    f = fourier_sqrt_project(n, None, top_windows=3)
    if f is not None:
        f = mix_noisy(f, rng, 0.02)
        append_unique(starts, f)

    if best_f is not None:
        fb = interpolate_sequence(best_f, n)
        if fb is not None:
            b = convolve_fft(fb)
            max_iter = max(4, min(25, 2500 // max(1, n)))
            f = fourier_alternating_candidate(
                n, rng, beta=0.6, max_iter=max_iter,
                budget_s=3.0, seed_b=b
            )
            if f is not None:
                f = mix_noisy(f, rng, 0.02)
                append_unique(starts, f)

    if not starts:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    scored = []
    for f in starts:
        sc = local_score(f)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, f))

    if not scored:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    out = []
    for sc, f in scored[:6]:
        out.append(f)
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP best-response local search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def make_conv_template(n):
    """
    Precompute the sparsity pattern for convolution f*g.
    data holds integer offsets k - i, later replaced by f[offset] * scale.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = 2 * n - 1

    k = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int32)
    js = np.maximum(0, k.astype(np.int64) - n + 1).astype(np.int32)
    je = np.minimum(n - 1, k.astype(np.int64)).astype(np.int32)
    counts = (je.astype(np.int64) - js.astype(np.int64) + 1).astype(np.int64)

    total = int(counts.sum())
    cum = np.cumsum(counts)

    indptr = np.zeros(m + 1, dtype=np.int32)
    indptr[1:] = cum.astype(np.int32)

    starts = np.repeat(cum - counts, counts).astype(np.int32)
    local = np.arange(total, dtype=np.int32) - starts
    row = np.repeat(k, counts).astype(np.int32)
    col = (js[row] + local).astype(np.int32)
    data = row.astype(np.float64) - col.astype(np.float64)

    A = csr_matrix((data, col, indptr), shape=(m, n))
    edge_offsets = A.data.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    A.data.fill(0.0)

    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    return A, edge_offsets, obj, bounds, m


def solve_direction_lp(template, f, n, M, time_limit):
    """
    Solve max sum(g) s.t. (f*g)_k <= M, g >= 0.
    Returns primal x or None.
    """
    if template is None:
        return None
    A, edge_offsets, obj, bounds, m = template
    scale = float(n)

    A.data[:] = f[edge_offsets]
    A.data *= scale
    b = np.full(m, M * scale * scale, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = linprog(
            obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(1.0, time_limit))}
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True}
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None
    if int(res.status) not in (0, 2):
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    return x


def make_safe_ts(b_f, c, b_g, M):
    """
    Safe step sizes along f_t = (1-t)f + t g for which all
    (f*f)_k(t) <= M (using c=f*g and b_g=g*g).
    """
    m = c.size
    tol = 1e-10 * max(1.0, float(M))
    alpha = 2.0 * (c - M)
    beta = b_g + b_f - 2.0 * c

    roots = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = beta > 1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
        ap = alpha[pos]
        bp = beta[pos]
        r = np.where(ap < -tol, -ap / bp, 0.0)
        roots[pos] = np.minimum(roots[pos], r)

    roots = np.maximum(roots, 0.0)
    t_safe = float(np.min(roots))
    if not np.isfinite(t_safe) or t_safe <= 1e-8:
        return []

    if t_safe > 1.0:
        t_safe = 1.0
    t_safe = t_safe * 0.999999

    lo = max(1e-8, t_safe * 1e-6)
    if lo >= t_safe:
        lo = t_safe * 0.5

    parts = []
    try:
        parts.append(np.logspace(math.log10(lo), math.log10(t_safe), 12))
    except Exception:
        pass
    parts.append(np.linspace(lo, t_safe, 12))

    ts = np.unique(np.concatenate(parts))
    ts = ts[(ts > 0.0) & (ts <= t_safe)]
    return ts.tolist()


def optimize_lp(n, f_init, time_budget, rng, template):
    """
    LP best-response iteration from one initial sequence.
    Returns best normalized sequence and local score found.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    f = normalize_sequence(f_init)
    if f is None or f.size != n:
        f = interpolate_sequence(f_init, n)
    if f is None:
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    if template is None:
        try:
            template = make_conv_template(n)
        except Exception:
            return f, score_normalized(f)

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_s = score_normalized(f)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break

        b_f = convolve_fft(f)
        M = float(np.max(b_f)) if b_f.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        x = solve_direction_lp(
            template, f, n, M, min(90.0, max(1.0, rem - 2.0))
        )
        if x is None:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 4:
                break
            f = mix_noisy(best_f, rng, 0.3)
            if f is None:
                f = best_f.copy()
            continue

        s_star = float(np.sum(x))
        if s_star <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 10:
                break
            eps = min(0.7, 0.2 + 0.1 * stall)
            f = mix_noisy(best_f, rng, eps)
            if f is None:
                f = best_f.copy()
            continue

        g = x / s_star
        g = normalize_sequence(g)
        if g is None:
            f = mix_noisy(best_f, rng, 0.3)
            if f is None:
                f = best_f.copy()
            continue

        c = cross_convolve_fft(f, g)
        b_g = convolve_fft(g)

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_s = best_s

        ts = make_safe_ts(b_f, c, b_g, M)
        if ts:
            Ts = np.asarray(ts, dtype=np.float64)
            vals = (
                (1.0 - Ts)[:, None] ** 2 * b_f[None, :]
                + (2.0 * Ts * (1.0 - Ts))[:, None] * c[None, :]
                + (Ts[:, None] ** 2) * b_g[None, :]
            )
            maxvals = np.max(vals, axis=1)
            scores = 2.0 * float(n) * maxvals
            idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
            t = float(Ts[idx])
            f_new = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
            f_new = normalize_sequence(f_new)
            if f_new is not None:
                sc = score_normalized(f_new)
                if sc < best_cand_s:
                    best_cand_s = sc
                    best_cand = f_new

        # Also try the endpoint g and midpoint, which can be useful if the
        # safe interval is tiny but g itself is strong.
        sc_g = score_normalized(g)
        if sc_g < best_cand_s:
            best_cand_s = sc_g
            best_cand = g.copy()

        f_half = 0.5 * f + 0.5 * g
        f_half = normalize_sequence(f_half)
        if f_half is not None:
            sc_half = score_normalized(f_half)
            if sc_half < best_cand_s:
                best_cand_s = sc_half
                best_cand = f_half

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_s < best_s - 1e-12:
            f = best_cand.copy()
            best_f = f.copy()
            best_s = best_cand_s
            stall = 0
            if it <= 5 or it % 20 == 0:
                print("     n=%d LP iter=%d score=%.6f s_star=%.4f"
                      % (n, it, best_s, s_star), flush=True)
        else:
            stall += 1
            f = mix_noisy(best_f, rng, min(0.5, 0.15 + 0.05 * stall))
            if f is None:
                f = best_f.copy()

    return best_f, float(best_s)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# FFT-based smooth/pseudo-gradient polishing
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def smooth_weights(q, beta):
    """Softmax weights over coefficients q."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(qmax):
        return np.ones(q.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(q.size)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(q.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(q.size)

    z = np.minimum(0.0, beta * (q - qmax))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(q.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(q.size)
    return e / s


def corr_p_f(p, f):
    """
    For p over convolution lags 0..2n-2 and f length n, compute
    g_i = 2 * sum_k p_k f_{k-i}.
    This is the gradient of sum_k p_k (f*f)_k w.r.t. f.
    """
    n = f.size
    m = p.size
    need = m + n - 1
    L = next_pow2(need)

    P = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    P[:m] = p[::-1]
    F = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    F[:n] = f

    C = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(P) * np.fft.rfft(F), L)[:need]
    # g_i = C[m-1 - i], for i=0..n-1 => indices m-1, m-2, ..., n-1.
    g = C[n - 1:m][:1][::-1] if False else C[n - 1:m][::-1]
    return 2.0 * g


def gradient_polish(f_init, time_budget, rng):
    """
    Fast local polishing by exponential/multiplicative updates in log-space.
    Uses smoothed active-set subgradients of max_k (f*f)_k.
    """
    f = normalize_sequence(f_init)
    if f is None:
        f = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    n = f.size

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_s = score_normalized(f)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    logf = np.log(f + 1e-300)
    logf = logf - float(np.mean(logf))

    current_beta = max(1000.0, 50.0 * float(n))
    stall = 0
    mults = np.array([1e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 1e-1, 3e-1, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0],
                     dtype=np.float64)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = convolve_fft(f)
        bmax = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
            break

        beta = max(current_beta, 20.0 / (bmax + 1e-15), 100.0)
        p = smooth_weights(b, beta)
        gf = corr_p_f(p, f)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gf)):
            break

        mean_wf = float(np.dot(f, gf))
        d = gf - mean_wf
        d = d - float(np.dot(f, d))  # constants in log-space are irrelevant

        exact_d = f * d
        exact_d = exact_d - float(np.dot(f, exact_d))

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_s = best_s
        best_log = None

        dirs = (d, exact_d)
        for dir in dirs:
            dir = np.asarray(dir, dtype=np.float64)
            if dir.size != n:
                continue
            dir = dir - float(np.dot(f, dir))
            norm_dir = float(np.linalg.norm(dir))
            max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(dir))) if dir.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(norm_dir) or norm_dir <= 1e-300:
                continue

            base = 1.0 / norm_dir
            if max_abs > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(max_abs):
                base = max(base, 1.0 / max_abs)
            if not np.isfinite(base) or base <= 0.0:
                continue

            for mu in mults:
                lr = base * float(mu)
                if lr <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(lr):
                    continue
                new_log = logf - lr * dir
                new_log = new_log - float(np.mean(new_log))
                new_f = softmax_from_log(new_log)
                sc = score_normalized(new_f)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_s - 1e-12:
                    best_cand_s = sc
                    best_cand = new_f.copy()
                    best_log = new_log.copy()

        if best_cand is not None:
            f = best_cand
            logf = best_log
            best_f = f.copy()
            best_s = best_cand_s
            stall = 0
            if stall and stall % 50 == 0:
                print("     grad polished score=%.6f" % best_s, flush=True)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 20 == 0:
                if current_beta < 1e7:
                    current_beta *= 2.0
                else:
                    current_beta /= 4.0
            if stall % 75 == 0:
                eps = min(0.5, 0.1 + 0.05 * (stall // 75))
                f = mix_noisy(best_f, rng, eps)
                if f is None:
                    f = best_f.copy()
                logf = np.log(f + 1e-300)
                logf = logf - float(np.mean(logf))
            if stall > 300:
                break

    return best_f, float(best_s)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Length-level search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

MAX_LP_N = 2048


def search_length(n, budget, deadline, rng, best_f, best_score,
                  given_f, lp_frac=0.55):
    """
    Run LP and/or gradient search for a fixed length n within a budget.
    Returns (best_f, best_score) globally improved.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    local_deadline = min(float(deadline), t0 + float(budget))

    starts = make_starts(n, rng, best_f, given_f)
    if not starts:
        starts = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    # ---------------- LP phase ----------------
    lp_budget = float(budget) * float(max(0.0, min(1.0, lp_frac)))
    if lp_budget > 25.0 and n <= MAX_LP_N:
        lp_deadline = min(local_deadline, t0 + lp_budget)
        template = None
        try:
            template = make_conv_template(n)
        except MemoryError:
            print("     memory error building LP template for n=%d" % n,
                  flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("     LP template error n=%d: %s" % (n, e), flush=True)

        if template is not None:
            num_starts = min(3, len(starts))
            if num_starts == 1:
                weights = [1.0]
            elif num_starts == 2:
                weights = [0.6, 0.4]
            else:
                weights = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]

            for idx in range(num_starts):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= lp_deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                tb = lp_budget * weights[idx]
                tb = min(tb, lp_deadline - now - 1.0)
                if tb < 3.0:
                    continue

                print("     n=%d LP start %d/%d budget=%.1fs"
                      % (n, idx + 1, num_starts, tb), flush=True)
                f_res, s_res = optimize_lp(n, starts[idx], tb, rng, template)
                if np.isfinite(s_res) and s_res < best_score:
                    best_score = float(s_res)
                    best_f = np.asarray(f_res, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    print("     new local best n=%d score=%.6f"
                          % (best_f.size, best_score), flush=True)

            try:
                del template
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ---------------- Gradient phase ----------------
    grad_deadline = local_deadline

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < grad_deadline - 10.0:
        init = interpolate_sequence(best_f, n)
        if init is None:
            init = starts[0]
        tb = min(300.0, grad_deadline - now - 5.0)
        if tb > 5.0:
            print("     n=%d gradient polish budget=%.1fs" % (n, tb),
                  flush=True)
            f_res, s_res = gradient_polish(init, tb, rng)
            if np.isfinite(s_res) and s_res < best_score:
                best_score = float(s_res)
                best_f = np.asarray(f_res, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("     new local best n=%d score=%.6f"
                      % (best_f.size, best_score), flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < grad_deadline - 20.0:
        for idx in range(1, min(3, len(starts))):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= grad_deadline - 10.0:
                break
            tb = min(120.0, grad_deadline - now - 5.0)
            if tb < 5.0:
                continue
            f_res, s_res = gradient_polish(starts[idx], tb, rng)
            if np.isfinite(s_res) and s_res < best_score:
                best_score = float(s_res)
                best_f = np.asarray(f_res, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("     new local best (start %d) n=%d score=%.6f"
                      % (idx, best_f.size, best_score), flush=True)

    return best_f, best_score


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Final sequence packaging
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def prepare_sequence(f):
    """Pack a sequence for evaluate_sequence: non-negative, finite, normalized."""
    arr = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1.0
    else:
        arr = arr / s

    out = []
    for v in arr:
        vv = float(v)
        if vv < 0.0:
            vv = 0.0
        if vv > 1000.0:
            vv = 1000.0
        out.append(vv)
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a low-scoring non-negative sequence using a hybrid
    LP + FFT polishing + Fourier square-root strategy.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    reserve = 25.0 if total_budget > 100.0 else 5.0
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, total_budget - reserve)

    best_f = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    given_f = None

    # Use provided parent construction if available.
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if raw is not None:
            given_f = normalize_sequence(raw)
            if given_f is not None:
                s = local_score(given_f)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    best_f = given_f.copy()
                    best_score = float(s)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_f is None:
        best_f = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0
        best_score = local_score(best_f)

    print("initial best n=%d local_score=%.6f"
          % (len(best_f), best_score), flush=True)

    # Length plan.  The first length is the parent length; then try several
    # larger lengths.  Larger lengths get mostly gradient polishing.
    n0 = int(best_f.size)
    tasks = []
    if n0 <= 2048:
        tasks.append((n0, 0.60, 400.0))
    else:
        tasks.append((n0, 0.0, 250.0))

    additional = (
        (1536, 0.50, 150.0),
        (2048, 0.50, 250.0),
        (3072, 0.0, 180.0),
        (4096, 0.0, 180.0),
        (8192, 0.0, 140.0),
    )
    processed = set([n0])
    for n, lpf, bud in additional:
        if n not in processed:
            tasks.append((n, lpf, bud))
            processed.add(n)

    for n, lpf, bud in tasks:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 15.0:
            break
        b = min(float(bud), deadline - now - 5.0)
        if b < 25.0:
            continue

        print("\n== length n=%d, budget=%.1fs, current_best=%.6f"
              % (n, b, best_score), flush=True)
        best_f, best_score = search_length(
            n, b, deadline, rng, best_f, best_score, given_f, lpf
        )
        print("after n=%d best local_score=%.6f" % (n, best_score),
              flush=True)

    # Final polishing at the current best length, if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < deadline - 20.0 and best_f is not None:
        tb = min(180.0, deadline - now - 3.0)
        if tb > 10.0:
            print("\nfinal polish budget=%.1fs" % tb, flush=True)
            f_res, s_res = gradient_polish(best_f, tb, rng)
            if np.isfinite(s_res) and s_res < best_score:
                best_score = float(s_res)
                best_f = np.asarray(f_res, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print("final polish improved to %.6f" % best_score,
                      flush=True)

    # Final selection using the true evaluator when available.
    candidates = []
    if best_f is not None:
        candidates.append(best_f)
    if given_f is not None:
        candidates.append(given_f)
    candidates.append(np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_eval = float("inf")

    for cand in candidates:
        seq = prepare_sequence(cand)
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_eval:
            best_eval = sc
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = prepare_sequence(best_f if best_f is not None else
                                    np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0)

    print("FINAL selected length=%d local_best=%.6f true_eval=%.6f"
          % (len(best_seq), best_score, best_eval), flush=True)
    return best_seq
```
